{
  "server": "westd-16980",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\ntok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('/root/autodl-tmp/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct', trust_remote_code=True)\n\n# SciERC entity types\nscierc_types = ['Task', 'Method', 'Material', 'Metric', 'OtherScientificTerm', 'Generic']\nprint('=== SciERC Entity Types (Llama-3.1-8B) ===')\nfirst_tokens = {}\nfor t in scierc_types:\n    ids = tok.encode(t, add_special_tokens=False)\n    first_id = ids[0]\n    print(f'  {t}: first_token_id={first_id}, all_ids={ids}')\n    if first_id in first_tokens:\n        print(f'  *** COLLISION: {t} collides with {first_tokens[first_id]}!')\n    first_tokens[first_id] = t\n\n# DocRED entity types\ndocred_types = ['PER', 'ORG', 'LOC', 'TIME', 'NUM', 'MISC']\nprint()\nprint('=== DocRED Entity Types (Llama-3.1-8B) ===')\nfirst_tokens2 = {}\nfor t in docred_types:\n    ids = tok.encode(t, add_special_tokens=False)\n    first_id = ids[0]\n    print(f'  {t}: first_token_id={first_id}, all_ids={ids}')\n    if first_id in first_tokens2:\n        print(f'  *** COLLISION: {t} collides with {first_tokens2[first_id]}!')\n    first_tokens2[first_id] = t\n\n# Check chat template\nprint()\nprint('=== Chat Template Check ===')\nmsgs = [{'role': 'system', 'content': 'You are helpful.'}, {'role': 'user', 'content': 'Hello'}]\ntext = tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\nprint(text[:300])\nprint()\nprint(f'pad_token: {tok.pad_token}')\nprint(f'eos_token: {tok.eos_token}')\nprint(f'has chat_template: {tok.chat_template is not None}')\n\"\n",
  "account": "root"
}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
All first tokens are unique for both SciERC and DocRED entity types with the Llama tokenizer. No collisions. 

Now let me also check a couple more things:
1. The `_chat_template_kwargs` function - for Llama it returns `{}`, which is correct since Llama doesn't have `enable_thinking` parameter
2. The `pad_token` is None for Llama - the code handles this with `if tokenizer.pad_token is None: tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token`
3. XGrammar compatibility with Llama tokenizer - `xgr.TokenizerInfo.from_huggingface()` should work since it uses the HuggingFace tokenizer API


4. Llama's chat template uses special tokens like `<|begin_of_text|>` and `<|start_header_id|>`, which `tokenizer.apply_chat_template()` handles automatically.

I should verify that MISC on DocRED with first_token_id=44 doesn't cause issues, though the collision check already passed. I also need to confirm that Llama's vocab size won't be problematic for XGrammar and that `xgr.TokenizerInfo.from_huggingface` actually works with the Llama tokenizer.

Now checking the `--model_path` argument - it defaults to None but the code has a fallback that sets it to a specific path if not provided, so it's optional at the command line level. The batch script explicitly passes the model path, so if it's None the code would fail—but that's not an issue since it's always provided at runtime. Looking at my findings: the model loading is flexible with `AutoTokenizer` and `AutoModelForCausalLM`, and the prompt templates use `apply_chat_template()` which adapts to each model, with `enable_thinking=False` only applied for Qwen models.

The constrained decoding with XGrammar is tokenizer-agnostic, and I've verified that both SciERC and DocRED have unique first tokens across their entity types with the Llama tokenizer—plus the `compile_type_constraints()` method validates this at runtime and fails loudly if there's a collision. For the pad token issue, Llama has `pad_token=None` but the code handles this by checking and setting it if needed.

The chat template format is also handled automatically through `apply_chat_template()`, so Llama's `<|start_header_id|>` format works fine. There's a docstring in `type_mask_processor.py` with hardcoded token IDs for documentation purposes, but that's not functional code. Now I should check whether `model_path` has any hardcoded defaults in the main entry point.

offset:end - offset + 1])\\n                        gold_entities.append({\\\"text\\\": mention, \\\"type\\\": etype})\\n\\n                gold_relations = []\\n                if sent_idx < len(rel_all):\\n                    span_type = {}\\n                    if sent_idx < len(ner_all):\\n                        for s, e, et in ner_all[sent_idx]:\\n                            span_type[(s, e)] = et\\n                    for h_s, h_e, t_s, t_e, rtype_raw in rel_all[sent_idx]:\\n                        head_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[h_s - offset:h_e - offset + 1])\\n                        tail_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[t_s - offset:t_e - offset + 1])\\n                        head_type = span_type.get((h_s, h_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        tail_type = span_type.get((t_s, t_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        rtype = RAW_TO_CANONICAL.get(rtype_raw, rtype_raw)\\n                        gold_relations.append({\\n                            \\\"head\\\": head_text, \\\"tail\\\": tail_text, \\\"type\\\": rtype,\\n                            \\\"head_type\\\": head_type, \\\"tail_type\\\": tail_type,\\n \\n\\n... try:\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            out_p2 = model.generate(\\n                **inp_p2, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n                do_sample=False, logits_processor=procs_p2,\\n            )\\n    except AssertionError:\\n        import warnings\\n        warnings.warn(\\\"XGrammar assertion failed in S2 Pass 2 — falling back to grammar-only\\\")\\n        inp_p2_retry =     with torch.no_grad():\\n            out_p2 = model.generate(\\n                **inp_p2_retry, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n                do_sample=False, logits_processor=fallback_p2,\\n            )\\n    raw_p2 = tokenizer.decode(out_p2[0][plen_p2:], skip_special_tokens=True).strip()\\n    parsed_p2 = parse_model_output(raw_p2)\\n\\n    relations = parsed_p2.get(\\\"relations\\\", []) if parsed_p2 else []\\n\\n    combined_raw = f\\\"[Pass1] {raw_p1}\\\\n[Pass2] {raw_p2}\\\"\\n    return combined_raw, {\\\"entities\\\": entities, \\\"relations\\\": relations}\\n\\n\\n\\n# -- Resume --\\n\\ndef load_processed_ids(output_path, mode=\\\"full_s2\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load doc_ids already processed in the output file for --resume.\\n    For full_s2: only count samples where Pass 2 completed (has relations key in parsed_output).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    processed = set()\\n    if not os.path.exists(output_path):\\n        return processed\\n    with open(output_path) as f:\\n ## Llama-3.1-8B 兼容性报告

### 结论：开箱即用，零代码修改

只需传 `--model_path` 指向 Llama 路径即可。

---

### 逐项检查结果

**1. 模型加载 ✅**
- `--model_path` 是 CLI 参数，不是硬编码。默认值 fallback 到 Qwen 路径，但传参即覆盖
- 使用 `AutoTokenizer` + `AutoModelForCausalLM`，模型无关

**2. Prompt 模板 ✅**
- 全部通过 `tokenizer.apply_chat_template()` 构造，自动适配模型格式
- Qwen 特有逻辑仅在 `_chat_template_kwargs()` 中：检测到 `'qwen' in name` 时传 `enable_thinking=False`，非 Qwen 模型返回空 dict，不影响

**3. Constrained Decoding ✅**

| 组件 | 兼容性 | 原因 |
|------|--------|------|
| XGrammar | ✅ | `xgr.TokenizerInfo.from_huggingface()` 是 tokenizer 无关的 |
| TypeConstraintProcessor (first-token masking) | ✅ | 已验证 Llama-3.1-8B tokenizer 对所有 entity type 名称的 first token 唯一 |

Llama-3.1-8B first-token 验证结果：
```
SciERC:  Task=6396, Method=3607, Material=13721, Metric=55410, OtherScientificTerm=11663, Generic=20560  — 全部唯一 ✅
DocRED:  PER=9851, ORG=46027, LOC=12321, TIME=18621, NUM=17946, MISC=44  — 全部唯一 ✅
```

**4. pad_token ✅**
- Llama `pad_token=None`，代码已处理：`if tokenizer.pad_token is None: tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token`

**5. 配置文件 ✅**
- `ontology_constraints.json` 定义的是 schema 约束（entity types, relation types, valid pairs），与模型无关

---

### 推理命令模板

Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 已在服务器上：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct`

```bash

MODEL=/root/autodl-tmp/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct

python3 run_constrained_extraction.py \
  --mode full_s1 \
  --dataset scierc \
  --model_path $MODEL \
  --data_path data/processed_data/test.json \
  --constraints_path ontology_constraints.json \
  --output_path outputs/phase2_scierc_full_s1_llama.jsonl \
  --device cuda:0

# DocRED
python3 run_constrained_extraction.py \
  --mode full_s1 \
  --dataset docred \

  --constraints_path data/docred/ontology_constraints_docred.json \
  --output_path outputs/phase2_docred_full_s1_llama.jsonl \
  --device cuda:0
```
